<script>
    function togglePw() {
        const inp  = document.getElementById('password');
        const icon = document.getElementById('eye-icon');
        const show = inp.type === 'password';
        inp.type = show ? 'text' : 'password';
        icon.innerHTML = show
            ? '<path d="M17.94 17.94A10.07 10.07 0 0 1 12 20c-7 0-11-8-11-8a18.45 18.45 0 0 1 5.06-5.94M9.9 4.24A9.12 9.12 0 0 1 12 4c7 0 11 8 11 8a18.5 18.5 0 0 1-2.16 3.19m-6.72-1.07a3 3 0 1 1-4.24-4.24"/><line x1="1" y1="1" x2="23" y2="23"/>'
            : '<path d="M1 12s4-8 11-8 11 8 11 8-4 8-11 8-11-8-11-8z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/>';
    }
</script>

    <script>
        // Script Auto-tab untuk OTP (Kursor pindah otomatis setelah ketik angka)
        const inputs = document.querySelectorAll('.otp-input');
        inputs.forEach((input, index) => {
            input.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
                if (e.key >= 0 && e.key <= 9) {
                    if (index < inputs.length - 1) inputs[index + 1].focus();
                } else if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
                    if (index > 0) inputs[index - 1].focus();
                }
            });
        });
    </script>

<div
    x-data="{
        show: @js($show),
        focusables() {
            // All focusable element types...
            let selector = 'a, button, input:not([type=\'hidden\']), textarea, select, details, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\'-1\'])'
            return [...$el.querySelectorAll(selector)]
                // All non-disabled elements...
                .filter(el => ! el.hasAttribute('disabled'))
        },
        firstFocusable() { return this.focusables()[0] },
        lastFocusable() { return this.focusables().slice(-1)[0] },
        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },
        prevFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.prevFocusableIndex()] || this.lastFocusable() },
        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },
        prevFocusableIndex() { return Math.max(0, this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement)) -1 },
    }"
    x-init="$watch('show', value => {
        if (value) {
            document.body.classList.add('overflow-y-hidden');
            {{ $attributes->has('focusable') ? 'setTimeout(() => firstFocusable().focus(), 100)' : '' }}
        } else {
            document.body.classList.remove('overflow-y-hidden');
        }
    })"
    x-on:open-modal.window="$event.detail == '{{ $name }}' ? show = true : null"
    x-on:close-modal.window="$event.detail == '{{ $name }}' ? show = false : null"
    x-on:close.stop="show = false"
    x-on:keydown.escape.window="show = false"
    x-on:keydown.tab.prevent="$event.shiftKey || nextFocusable().focus()"
    x-on:keydown.shift.tab.prevent="prevFocusable().focus()"
    x-show="show"
    class="fixed inset-0 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-6 sm:px-0 z-50"
    style="display: {{ $show ? 'block' : 'none' }};"
>
    <div
        x-show="show"
        class="fixed inset-0 transform transition-all"
        x-on:click="show = false"
        x-transition:enter="ease-out duration-300"
        x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0"
        x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100"
        x-transition:leave="ease-in duration-200"
        x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100"
        x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0"
    >
        <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gray-500 opacity-75"></div>
    </div>

    <div

        x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 translate-y-4 sm:translate-y-0 sm:scale-95"

    <script>
        function updateFileName(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
            if (input.files.length) {
                display.textContent = input.files[0].name;
                display.style.color = "#333"; 
            } else {
                display.textContent = "Masukkan foto profil...";
                display.style.color = "#b0b0b0";
            }
        }
    </script>

<script>
    function confirmDelete(id) {
        if(confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus admin ini?')) {
            document.getElementById('delete-form-'+id).submit();
        }
    }
    function openFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }
    function closeFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'none';
    }
    window.onclick = function(event) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('filterModal');
        if (event.target == modal) closeFilterModal();
    }
    function syncCardVisual(checkboxElement) {
        let parentCard = checkboxElement.closest('.filter-checkbox-card');
        if (checkboxElement.checked) parentCard.classList.add('selected-box');
        else parentCard.classList.remove('selected-box');
    }
    function submitFormFilter() {
        document.getElementById('formFilterAdmin').submit();
    }
    document.getElementById('searchAdmin').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            submitFormFilter();
        }
    });
</script>

    <script>
        // Menandai penghapusan foto profil lama di database
        function hapusVisualFotoLama() {
            if(confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus foto profil saat ini?')) {
                const wadah = document.getElementById('wadahInputHapusFoto');
                const inputHidden = document.createElement('input');
                inputHidden.type = 'hidden';
                inputHidden.name = 'hapus_foto[]';
                inputHidden.value = "{{ $admin->galeri->id ?? '' }}";
                wadah.appendChild(inputHidden);

                const box = document.getElementById('boxFotoProfilLama');
                if(box) box.remove();
            }
        }

        // Render pratinjau instan foto baru yang dipilih
        function updateFileNameAndPreview(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
            const container = document.getElementById('imagePreviewContainer');
            
            if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
                display.textContent = input.files[0].name;
                display.style.color = "#333";
                container.innerHTML = '';

                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = function(e) {
                    const img = document.createElement('img');
                    img.src = e.target.result;
                    img.style.width = '100px';
                    img.style.height = '100px';
                    img.style.objectFit = 'cover';
                    img.style.borderRadius = '50%';
                    img.style.border = '2px dashed #A87C7C';
                    container.appendChild(img);
                };
                reader.readAsDataURL(input.files[0]);
            } else {
                display.textContent = "Pilih foto baru...";
                display.style.color = "#b0b0b0";
                container.innerHTML = '';
            }
        }

    <script>
        // Sediakan sebuah wadah (array) global untuk menampung semua file biner gambar
        let kumpulanFileBerita = [];

        function updateFileNameAndPreview(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
            const container = document.getElementById('imagePreviewContainer');

            if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
                // 1. Masukkan file yang baru dipilih ke dalam wadah array global kita
                for (let i = 0; i < input.files.length; i++) {
                    kumpulanFileBerita.push(input.files[i]);
                }

                // 2. Kosongkan container preview lama untuk digambar ulang semuanya
                container.innerHTML = '';

                // 3. Gunakan DataTransfer API untuk menggabungkan file ke dalam elemen input HTML asli
                const dataTransferObat = new DataTransfer();

                kumpulanFileBerita.forEach((file, index) => {
                    dataTransferObat.items.add(file);

                    // Render Preview Gambar masing-masing beserta tombol hapus satuan (opsional)
                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = function (e) {
                        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                        wrapper.style.position = 'relative';
                        wrapper.style.display = 'inline-block';

                        const img = document.createElement('img');
                        img.src = e.target.result;
                        img.style.maxWidth = '100px';
                        img.style.maxHeight = '100px';
                        img.style.objectFit = 'cover';
                        img.style.borderRadius = '6px';
                        img.style.border = '1px dashed #A87C7C';

                        wrapper.appendChild(img);
                        container.appendChild(wrapper);
                    };
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });

                // 4. TIMPA file asli di dalam input HTML dengan daftar gabungan yang baru
                input.files = dataTransferObat.files;

                // Perbarui teks informasi total file terpilih
                display.textContent = kumpulanFileBerita.length + " file dipilih (terakumulasi)";
                display.style.color = "#333";
            }
        }
    </script>
    {{-- Memuat Library Resmi TinyMCE dari CDN Cloud --}}

        referrerpolicy="origin"></script>

            ].join(' '),

            font_family_formats:

                                <input type="checkbox" name="status[]" value="tayang" onchange="syncCardVisual(this)" {{ in_array('tayang', request('status', [])) ? 'checked' : '' }}> 
                                <span class="checkbox-label">Tayang</span>
                            </label>
                            <label class="filter-checkbox-card {{ in_array('tidak tayang', request('status', [])) ? 'selected-box' : '' }}">

                                <span class="checkbox-label">Tidak Ditayangkan</span>
                            </label>

                    </div>

                            @endif
                        </div>
                    </td>
                    <td><strong>{{ $berita->judul }}</strong><br>
                        <span style="font-size:0.7rem; color:#888;">{{ \Illuminate\Support\Str::limit($berita->isi_berita, 60) }}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td>{{ $berita->user->name ?? 'Admin' }}</td>
                    <td>{{ $berita->created_at ? $berita->created_at->format('d/m/Y H:i') : '-' }}</td>
                    <td>
                        @if($berita->status == 'tayang')
                            <span class="badge-tayang">Tayang</span>
                        @else
                            <span class="badge-tidak-tayang">Tidak Ditayangkan</span>
                        @endif
                    </td>
                    <td class="news-actions">
                        <a href="{{ route('admin.edit-berita', $berita->id) }}" class="btn-edit-news">Edit</a>
                        <button class="btn-hapus-news" onclick="confirmDelete({{ $berita->id }})">Hapus</button>
                        <form id="delete-form-{{ $berita->id }}" action="{{ route('admin.hapus-berita', $berita->id) }}" method="POST" style="display:none;">
                            @csrf @method('DELETE')
                        </form>
                    </td>
                </tr>
                @empty
                <tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center">Belum ada data berita yang sesuai kriteria.</td></tr>
                @endforelse
            </tbody>
        </table>
        
        {{-- ALIGNMENT REPAIR: Sinkronisasi Tampilan Navigasi Pagination Berita --}}
        <div class="news-pagination">
            @if($beritas->total() > 0)
                <p>Menampilkan {{ $beritas->firstItem() }} - {{ $beritas->lastItem() }} dari {{ $beritas->total() }} berita</p>
            @else
                <p>Menampilkan 0 berita</p>
            @endif
            
            <div class="news-page-controls">
                @if ($beritas->onFirstPage())

                @endif

                <span class="active">{{ $beritas->currentPage() }}</span>

                @if ($beritas->hasMorePages())
                    <a href="{{ $beritas->nextPageUrl() }}"><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></a>
                @else
                    <span class="disabled"><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></span>
                @endif
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

<script>
    function confirmDelete(id) {
        if(confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus berita ini?')) {
            document.getElementById('delete-form-'+id).submit();
        }
    }

    function openFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }

    function closeFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'none';
    }

    window.onclick = function(event) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('filterModal');
        if (event.target == modal) {
            closeFilterModal();
        }
    }

    function syncCardVisual(checkboxElement) {
        let parentCard = checkboxElement.closest('.filter-checkbox-card');
        if (checkboxElement.checked) {
            parentCard.classList.add('selected-box');
        } else {
            parentCard.classList.remove('selected-box');
        }
    }

    // Koreksi trigger pengiriman form via javascript
    function submitFormFilter() {
        document.getElementById('formFilterNews').submit();
    }

    document.getElementById('searchBerita').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            submitFormFilter();
        }
    });

    // AUTOMATIC SLIDESHOW MULTI-FOTO BERITA (5 DETIK)
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
        const containers = document.querySelectorAll('.slideshow-container');
        containers.forEach(container => {
            const images = container.querySelectorAll('img');
            if (images.length > 1) {
                let currentIndex = 0;
                setInterval(() => {
                    images[currentIndex].classList.remove('active');
                    currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % images.length;
                    images[currentIndex].classList.add('active');
                }, 5000);
            }
        });
    });
</script>

<script>
    function confirmDelete(id) {
        if(confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus berita ini?')) {
            document.getElementById('delete-form-'+id).submit();
        }
    }

    function openFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }

    function closeFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'none';
    }

    window.onclick = function(event) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('filterModal');
        if (event.target == modal) {
            closeFilterModal();
        }
    }

    function syncCardVisual(checkboxElement) {
        let parentCard = checkboxElement.closest('.filter-checkbox-card');
        if (checkboxElement.checked) {
            parentCard.classList.add('selected-box');
        } else {
            parentCard.classList.remove('selected-box');
        }
    }

    // Koreksi trigger pengiriman form via javascript
    function submitFormFilter() {
        document.getElementById('formFilterNews').submit();
    }

    document.getElementById('searchBerita').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            submitFormFilter();
        }
    });

    // AUTOMATIC SLIDESHOW MULTI-FOTO BERITA (5 DETIK)
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
        const containers = document.querySelectorAll('.slideshow-container');
        containers.forEach(container => {
            const images = container.querySelectorAll('img');
            if (images.length > 1) {
                let currentIndex = 0;
                setInterval(() => {
                    images[currentIndex].classList.remove('active');
                    currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % images.length;
                    images[currentIndex].classList.add('active');
                }, 5000);
            }
        });
    });
</script>

    <script>
        // FUNGSI BARU: Memasukkan ID foto ke input hidden dan menyembunyikannya dari UI
        function tandaiHapusFoto(idFoto) {
            if (confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus gambar ini dari berita?')) {
                const wadah = document.getElementById('wadahInputHapusFoto');

                // Buat elemen input hidden baru
                const inputHidden = document.createElement('input');
                inputHidden.type = 'hidden';
                inputHidden.name = 'hapus_foto[]';
                inputHidden.value = idFoto;
                wadah.appendChild(inputHidden);

                // Hilangkan box foto dari tampilan layar
                const boxFoto = document.getElementById('foto-lama-box-' + idFoto);
                if (boxFoto) {
                    boxFoto.remove();
                }
            }
        }

        // Penanganan Akumulasi Gambar Baru
        let kumpulanFileBerita = [];

        function updateFileNameAndPreview(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
            const container = document.getElementById('imagePreviewContainer');

            if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
                for (let i = 0; i < input.files.length; i++) {
                    kumpulanFileBerita.push(input.files[i]);
                }

                container.innerHTML = '';
                const dataTransferObat = new DataTransfer();

                kumpulanFileBerita.forEach((file) => {
                    dataTransferObat.items.add(file);

                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = function (e) {
                        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                        wrapper.style.position = 'relative';
                        wrapper.style.display = 'inline-block';

                        const img = document.createElement('img');
                        img.src = e.target.result;
                        img.style.width = '100px';
                        img.style.height = '100px';
                        img.style.objectFit = 'cover';
                        img.style.borderRadius = '6px';
                        img.style.border = '1px dashed #A87C7C';

                        wrapper.appendChild(img);
                        container.appendChild(wrapper);
                    };
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });

                input.files = dataTransferObat.files;
                display.textContent = kumpulanFileBerita.length + " file baru dipilih (terakumulasi)";
                display.style.color = "#333";
            }
        }

    <script>
        // FUNGSI BARU: Memasukkan ID foto ke input hidden dan menyembunyikannya dari UI
        function tandaiHapusFoto(idFoto) {
            if(confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus gambar ini dari berita?')) {
                const wadah = document.getElementById('wadahInputHapusFoto');
                
                // Buat elemen input hidden baru
                const inputHidden = document.createElement('input');
                inputHidden.type = 'hidden';
                inputHidden.name = 'hapus_foto[]';
                inputHidden.value = idFoto;
                wadah.appendChild(inputHidden);

                // Hilangkan box foto dari tampilan layar
                const boxFoto = document.getElementById('foto-lama-box-' + idFoto);
                if(boxFoto) {
                    boxFoto.remove();
                }
            }
        }

        // Penanganan Akumulasi Gambar Baru
        let kumpulanFileBerita = [];

        function updateFileNameAndPreview(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
            const container = document.getElementById('imagePreviewContainer');
            
            if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
                for (let i = 0; i < input.files.length; i++) {
                    kumpulanFileBerita.push(input.files[i]);
                }

                container.innerHTML = '';
                const dataTransferObat = new DataTransfer();
                
                kumpulanFileBerita.forEach((file) => {
                    dataTransferObat.items.add(file);

                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = function(e) {
                        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                        wrapper.style.position = 'relative';
                        wrapper.style.display = 'inline-block';

                        const img = document.createElement('img');
                        img.src = e.target.result;
                        img.style.width = '100px';
                        img.style.height = '100px';
                        img.style.objectFit = 'cover';
                        img.style.borderRadius = '6px';
                        img.style.border = '1px dashed #A87C7C';

                        wrapper.appendChild(img);
                        container.appendChild(wrapper);
                    };
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });

                input.files = dataTransferObat.files;
                display.textContent = kumpulanFileBerita.length + " file baru dipilih (terakumulasi)";
                display.style.color = "#333";
            }
        }
    </script>

                        <button class="btn-hapus-event" onclick="confirmDelete({{ $event->id }})">Hapus</button>
                        <form id="delete-form-{{ $event->id }}" action="{{ route('admin.hapus-event', $event->id) }}" method="POST" style="display:none;">
                            @csrf @method('DELETE')
                        </form>
                    </td>
                </tr>
                @empty
                <tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;">Belum ada data event yang sesuai kriteria.</td></tr>
                @endforelse
            </tbody>

                @else
                    <span class="disabled"><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></span>
                @endif
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

<script>
    function confirmDelete(id) {
        if(confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus event ini?')) {
            document.getElementById('delete-form-'+id).submit();
        }
    }
    
    function openFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }

    // Perbaikan typo fungsi penutup modal window click
    function closeFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'none';
    }

    window.onclick = function(event) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('filterModal');
        if (event.target == modal) {
            closeFilterModal();
        }
    }

    function syncCardVisual(checkboxElement) {
        let parentCard = checkboxElement.closest('.filter-checkbox-card');
        if (checkboxElement.checked) {
            parentCard.classList.add('selected-box');
        } else {
            parentCard.classList.remove('selected-box');
        }
    }

    function submitFormFilter() {
        document.getElementById('formFilterEvent').submit();
    }

    document.getElementById('searchEvent').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            submitFormFilter();
        }
    });

    // AUTOMATIC SLIDESHOW MULTI-FOTO EVENT (5 DETIK)
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
        const containers = document.querySelectorAll('.slideshow-container');
        containers.forEach(container => {
            const images = container.querySelectorAll('img');
            if (images.length > 1) {
                let currentIndex = 0;
                setInterval(() => {
                    images[currentIndex].classList.remove('active');
                    currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % images.length;
                    images[currentIndex].classList.add('active');
                }, 5000);
            }
        });
    });
</script>

<script>
    function confirmDelete(id) {
        if(confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus event ini?')) {
            document.getElementById('delete-form-'+id).submit();
        }
    }
    
    function openFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }

    // Perbaikan typo fungsi penutup modal window click
    function closeFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'none';
    }

    window.onclick = function(event) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('filterModal');
        if (event.target == modal) {
            closeFilterModal();
        }
    }

    function syncCardVisual(checkboxElement) {
        let parentCard = checkboxElement.closest('.filter-checkbox-card');
        if (checkboxElement.checked) {
            parentCard.classList.add('selected-box');
        } else {
            parentCard.classList.remove('selected-box');
        }
    }

    function submitFormFilter() {
        document.getElementById('formFilterEvent').submit();
    }

    document.getElementById('searchEvent').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            submitFormFilter();
        }
    });

    // AUTOMATIC SLIDESHOW MULTI-FOTO EVENT (5 DETIK)
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
        const containers = document.querySelectorAll('.slideshow-container');
        containers.forEach(container => {
            const images = container.querySelectorAll('img');
            if (images.length > 1) {
                let currentIndex = 0;
                setInterval(() => {
                    images[currentIndex].classList.remove('active');
                    currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % images.length;
                    images[currentIndex].classList.add('active');
                }, 5000);
            }
        });
    });
</script>

    <script>
        // Fungsi Penanda Hapus Foto Sesuai Modul Berita
        function tandaiHapusFoto(idFoto) {
            if(confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus gambar ini dari event?')) {
                const wadah = document.getElementById('wadahInputHapusFoto');
                
                const inputHidden = document.createElement('input');
                inputHidden.type = 'hidden';
                inputHidden.name = 'hapus_foto[]';
                inputHidden.value = idFoto;
                wadah.appendChild(inputHidden);

                const boxFoto = document.getElementById('foto-lama-box-' + idFoto);
                if(boxFoto) {
                    boxFoto.remove();
                }
            }
        }

        // Penanganan Akumulasi File Baru Instan
        let kumpulanFileEvent = [];

        function updateFileNameAndPreview(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
            const container = document.getElementById('imagePreviewContainer');
            
            if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
                for (let i = 0; i < input.files.length; i++) {
                    kumpulanFileEvent.push(input.files[i]);
                }

                container.innerHTML = '';
                const dataTransferObat = new DataTransfer();
                
                kumpulanFileEvent.forEach((file) => {
                    dataTransferObat.items.add(file);

                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = function(e) {
                        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                        wrapper.style.position = 'relative';
                        wrapper.style.display = 'inline-block';

                        const img = document.createElement('img');
                        img.src = e.target.result;
                        img.style.width = '100px';
                        img.style.height = '100px';
                        img.style.objectFit = 'cover';
                        img.style.borderRadius = '6px';
                        img.style.border = '1px dashed #A87C7C';

                        wrapper.appendChild(img);
                        container.appendChild(wrapper);
                    };
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });

                input.files = dataTransferObat.files;
                display.textContent = kumpulanFileEvent.length + " file baru dipilih (terakumulasi)";
                display.style.color = "#333";
            }
        }
    </script>

    <script>
        let kumpulanFileKatalog = [];

        function updateFileNameAndPreview(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
            const container = document.getElementById('imagePreviewContainer');
            
            if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
                for (let i = 0; i < input.files.length; i++) {
                    kumpulanFileKatalog.push(input.files[i]);
                }

                container.innerHTML = '';
                const dataTransferObat = new DataTransfer();
                
                kumpulanFileKatalog.forEach((file) => {
                    dataTransferObat.items.add(file);

                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = function(e) {
                        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                        wrapper.style.position = 'relative';
                        wrapper.style.display = 'inline-block';

                        const img = document.createElement('img');
                        img.src = e.target.result;
                        img.style.width = '100px';
                        img.style.height = '100px';
                        img.style.objectFit = 'cover';
                        img.style.borderRadius = '6px';
                        img.style.border = '1px dashed #A87C7C';

                        wrapper.appendChild(img);
                        container.appendChild(wrapper);
                    };
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });

                input.files = dataTransferObat.files;
                display.textContent = kumpulanFileKatalog.length + " file dipilih (terakumulasi)";
                display.style.color = "#333";
            }
        }
    </script>

<script>
    function confirmDelete(id) {
        if(confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus katalog ini?')) {
            document.getElementById('delete-form-'+id).submit();
        }
    }

    function openFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }

    function closeFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'none';
    }

    window.onclick = function(event) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('filterModal');
        if (event.target == modal) {
            closeFilterModal();
        }
    }

    function syncCardVisual(checkboxElement) {
        let parentCard = checkboxElement.closest('.filter-checkbox-card');
        if (checkboxElement.checked) {
            parentCard.classList.add('selected-box');
        } else {
            parentCard.classList.remove('selected-box');
        }
    }

    function submitFormFilter() {
        document.getElementById('formFilterKatalog').submit();
    }

    // Jalankan submit form filter pencarian saat admin menekan tombol 'Enter' di keyboard
    document.getElementById('searchKatalog').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            submitFormFilter();
        }
    });

    // AUTOMATIC SLIDESHOW MULTI-FOTO PRODUK KATALOG (5 DETIK)
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
        const containers = document.querySelectorAll('.slideshow-container');
        containers.forEach(container => {
            const images = container.querySelectorAll('img');
            if (images.length > 1) {
                let currentIndex = 0;
                setInterval(() => {
                    images[currentIndex].classList.remove('active');
                    currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % images.length;
                    images[currentIndex].classList.add('active');
                }, 5000);
            }
        });
    });
</script>

                            @foreach($errors->all() as $error)
                                <li>{{ $error }}</li>
                            @endforeach

                    </div>

                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 15px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 10px;">
                        @foreach($katalog->galeri as $foto)
                            <div id="foto-lama-box-{{ $foto->id }}" style="position: relative; display: inline-block;">
                                <img src="{{ asset('storage/' . $foto->path) }}" style="width: 100px; height: 100px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid #c0c0c0;">

                            </div>

                        <label for="foto" class="btn-pilih-file">Pilih File</label>
                        <span class="file-name-display" id="fileNameDisplay">Kosongkan jika tidak ingin menambah gambar...</span>

    <script>
        function tandaiHapusFoto(idFoto) {
            if(confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus gambar ini dari katalog?')) {
                const wadah = document.getElementById('wadahInputHapusFoto');
                
                const inputHidden = document.createElement('input');
                inputHidden.type = 'hidden';
                inputHidden.name = 'hapus_foto[]';
                inputHidden.value = idFoto;
                wadah.appendChild(inputHidden);

                const boxFoto = document.getElementById('foto-lama-box-' + idFoto);
                if(boxFoto) {
                    boxFoto.remove();
                }
            }
        }

        let kumpulanFileKatalog = [];

        function updateFileNameAndPreview(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
            const container = document.getElementById('imagePreviewContainer');
            
            if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
                for (let i = 0; i < input.files.length; i++) {
                    kumpulanFileKatalog.push(input.files[i]);
                }

                container.innerHTML = '';
                const dataTransferObat = new DataTransfer();
                
                kumpulanFileKatalog.forEach((file) => {
                    dataTransferObat.items.add(file);

                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = function(e) {
                        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                        wrapper.style.position = 'relative';
                        wrapper.style.display = 'inline-block';

                        const img = document.createElement('img');
                        img.src = e.target.result;
                        img.style.width = '100px';
                        img.style.height = '100px';
                        img.style.objectFit = 'cover';
                        img.style.borderRadius = '6px';
                        img.style.border = '1px dashed #A87C7C';

                        wrapper.appendChild(img);
                        container.appendChild(wrapper);
                    };
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });

                input.files = dataTransferObat.files;
                display.textContent = kumpulanFileKatalog.length + " file baru dipilih (terakumulasi)";
                display.style.color = "#333";
            }
        }
    </script>

">

        <p style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#475569;margin-bottom:8px;">Server Error</p>
        <h2 style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:#E53935;margin-bottom:10px;">Format Data Tidak Sesuai</h2>
        <p style="font-size:13.5px;color:#475569;line-height:1.65;margin-bottom:16px;">Periksa kembali data yang Anda masukkan:</p>

<script>
    function openFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }

    function closeFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'none';
    }

    window.onclick = function(event) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('filterModal');
        if (event.target == modal) {
            closeFilterModal();
        }
    }

    function syncCardVisual(checkboxElement) {
        let parentCard = checkboxElement.closest('.filter-checkbox-card');
        if (checkboxElement.checked) {
            parentCard.classList.add('selected-box');
        } else {
            parentCard.classList.remove('selected-box');
        }
    }

    function submitFormFilter() {
        document.getElementById('formFilterLog').submit();
    }

    document.getElementById('searchLog').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            submitFormFilter();
        }
    });
</script>

<script>
    function previewAvatar(input) {
        const label = document.getElementById('avatarNameDisplay');
        const preview = document.getElementById('avatarPreview');
        
        if (input.files && input.files[0]) {
            label.textContent = input.files[0].name;
            label.style.color = "#333";
            
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function(e) {
                preview.src = e.target.result;
            }
            reader.readAsDataURL(input.files[0]);
        }
    }
</script>

    <script>
        // Logika hapus foto header lama (Client-side effect)
        function tandaiHapusHeader(idFoto) {
            if(confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus gambar ini dari slideshow header utama?')) {
                const wadah = document.getElementById('wadahInputHapusHeader');
                const inputHidden = document.createElement('input');
                inputHidden.type = 'hidden';
                inputHidden.name = 'hapus_header[]';
                inputHidden.value = idFoto;
                wadah.appendChild(inputHidden);

                const boxFoto = document.getElementById('foto-lama-box-' + idFoto);
                if(boxFoto) { boxFoto.remove(); }
            }
        }

        // Live preview penumpukan file baru dinamis
        let tumpukanHeaderBaru = [];
        function updateHeaderPreview(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('headerNameDisplay');
            const container = document.getElementById('headerPreviewContainer');
            
            if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
                for (let i = 0; i < input.files.length; i++) {
                    tumpukanHeaderBaru.push(input.files[i]);
                }

                container.innerHTML = '';
                const dt = new DataTransfer();
                
                tumpukanHeaderBaru.forEach((file) => {
                    dt.items.add(file);
                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = function(e) {
                        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                        wrapper.style.position = 'relative';
                        
                        const img = document.createElement('img');
                        img.src = e.target.result;
                        img.style.width = '120px';
                        img.style.height = '75px';
                        img.style.objectFit = 'cover';
                        img.style.borderRadius = '6px';
                        img.style.border = '1px dashed #A87C7C';

                        wrapper.appendChild(img);
                        container.appendChild(wrapper);
                    };
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });

                input.files = dt.files;
                display.textContent = tumpukanHeaderBaru.length + " file header baru dipilih (terakumulasi)";
                display.style.color = "#333";
            }
        }
    </script>
@endsection@extends('layouts.form')

    <script>
        function tandaiHapusFoto(idFoto) {
            if(confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus foto ini dari profil pelatih?')) {
                const wadah = document.getElementById('wadahInputHapusFoto');
                const inputHidden = document.createElement('input');
                inputHidden.type = 'hidden';
                inputHidden.name = 'hapus_foto[]';
                inputHidden.value = idFoto;
                wadah.appendChild(inputHidden);

                const boxFoto = document.getElementById('foto-lama-box-' + idFoto);
                if(boxFoto) boxFoto.remove();
            }
        }

        let kumpulanFilePelatih = [];
        function updateFileNameAndPreview(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
            const container = document.getElementById('imagePreviewContainer');
            
            if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
                for (let i = 0; i < input.files.length; i++) {
                    kumpulanFilePelatih.push(input.files[i]);
                }
                container.innerHTML = '';
                const dataTransferObat = new DataTransfer();
                kumpulanFilePelatih.forEach((file) => {
                    dataTransferObat.items.add(file);
                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = function(e) {
                        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                        wrapper.style.display = 'inline-block';
                        const img = document.createElement('img');
                        img.src = e.target.result;
                        img.style.width = '100px'; img.style.height = '100px'; img.style.objectFit = 'cover'; img.style.borderRadius = '6px'; img.style.border = '1px dashed #A87C7C';
                        wrapper.appendChild(img); container.appendChild(wrapper);
                    };
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });
                input.files = dataTransferObat.files;
                display.textContent = kumpulanFilePelatih.length + " file baru dipilih (terakumulasi)";
                display.style.color = "#333";
            }
        }
    </script>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label>Foto Profil / Kegiatan Pengurus Saat Ini (Klik tombol merah untuk menghapus)</label>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 15px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 10px;">
                        @foreach($pengurus->galeri as $foto)
                            <div id="foto-lama-box-{{ $foto->id }}" style="position: relative; display: inline-block;">
                                <img src="{{ asset('storage/' . $foto->path) }}" style="width: 100px; height: 100px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid #c0c0c0;">
                                <button type="button" class="btn-hapus-foto-lama" onclick="tandaiHapusFoto({{ $foto->id }})" title="Hapus gambar ini">
                                    <i class="fas fa-times"></i>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        @endforeach
                    </div>
                </div>
                @endif

                <div id="wadahInputHapusFoto"></div>

                {{-- Tambah Foto Baru --}}
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label>Unggah Foto Tambahan Baru (Bisa pilih lebih dari satu)</label>
                    <div class="custom-file-wrapper">
                        <label for="foto" class="btn-pilih-file">Pilih File</label>
                        <span class="file-name-display" id="fileNameDisplay">Kosongkan jika tidak ingin menambah foto...</span>
                        <input type="file" id="foto" name="foto[]" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/jpg" style="display:none" onchange="updateFileNameAndPreview(this)" multiple>
                    </div>
                    <div class="form-helper">Format: JPG, PNG. Maks 2MB per file.</div>
                    <div id="imagePreviewContainer" style="display: flex; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 12px;"></div>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="nama">Nama Lengkap</label>
                    <input type="text" id="nama" name="nama" value="{{ old('nama', $pengurus->nama) }}" placeholder="Masukkan nama lengkap pengurus..." required>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="jabatan">Jabatan</label>
                    <select id="jabatan" name="jabatan" required>
                        <option value="Ketua" {{ old('jabatan', $pengurus->jabatan) == 'Ketua' ? 'selected' : '' }}>Ketua</option>
                        <option value="Sekretaris" {{ old('jabatan', $pengurus->jabatan) == 'Sekretaris' ? 'selected' : '' }}>Sekretaris</option>
                        <option value="Bendahara" {{ old('jabatan', $pengurus->jabatan) == 'Bendahara' ? 'selected' : '' }}>Bendahara</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="instagram">Instagram</label>
                    <input type="text" id="instagram" name="instagram" value="{{ old('instagram', $pengurus->instagram) }}" placeholder="Contoh: @username">
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="hp">No Handphone</label>
                    <input type="text" id="hp" name="no_hp" value="{{ old('no_hp', $pengurus->no_handphone) }}" placeholder="Masukkan no handphone pengurus..." required>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="status">Status</label>
                    <select id="status" name="status" required>
                        <option value="aktif" {{ old('status', $pengurus->status) == 'aktif' ? 'selected' : '' }}>Aktif</option>
                        <option value="nonaktif" {{ old('status', $pengurus->status) == 'nonaktif' ? 'selected' : '' }}>Nonaktif</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        // Fungsi Pelacak Tombol Hapus Foto Lama
        function tandaiHapusFoto(idFoto) {
            if(confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus foto ini dari profil pengurus?')) {
                const wadah = document.getElementById('wadahInputHapusFoto');
                
                const inputHidden = document.createElement('input');
                inputHidden.type = 'hidden';
                inputHidden.name = 'hapus_foto[]';
                inputHidden.value = idFoto;
                wadah.appendChild(inputHidden);

                const boxFoto = document.getElementById('foto-lama-box-' + idFoto);
                if(boxFoto) {
                    boxFoto.remove();
                }
            }
        }

        // Penanganan Akumulasi File Baru Live Preview
        let kumpulanFilePengurus = [];

        function updateFileNameAndPreview(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
            const container = document.getElementById('imagePreviewContainer');
            
            if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
                for (let i = 0; i < input.files.length; i++) {
                    kumpulanFilePengurus.push(input.files[i]);
                }

                container.innerHTML = '';
                const dataTransferObat = new DataTransfer();
                
                kumpulanFilePengurus.forEach((file) => {
                    dataTransferObat.items.add(file);

                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = function(e) {
                        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                        wrapper.style.position = 'relative';
                        wrapper.style.display = 'inline-block';

                        const img = document.createElement('img');
                        img.src = e.target.result;
                        img.style.width = '100px';
                        img.style.height = '100px';
                        img.style.objectFit = 'cover';
                        img.style.borderRadius = '6px';
                        img.style.border = '1px dashed #A87C7C';

                        wrapper.appendChild(img);
                        container.appendChild(wrapper);
                    };
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });

                input.files = dataTransferObat.files;
                display.textContent = kumpulanFilePengurus.length + " file baru dipilih (terakumulasi)";
                display.style.color = "#333";
            }
        }
    </script>

<script>
    function confirmDelete(jenis, id) {
        if(confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus data ' + jenis + ' ini?')) {
            document.getElementById('delete-' + jenis + '-' + id).submit();
        }
    }

    // JAVASCRIPT ENGINE AUTOMATIC SLIDESHOW (5 DETIK)
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
        const containers = document.querySelectorAll('.slideshow-container');

        containers.forEach(container => {
            const images = container.querySelectorAll('img');
            if (images.length > 1) {
                let currentIndex = 0;

                setInterval(() => {
                    images[currentIndex].classList.remove('active');
                    currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % images.length;
                    images[currentIndex].classList.add('active');
                }, 5000);
            }
        });
    });
</script>

<script>
    function confirmDelete(jenis, id) {
        if(confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus data ' + jenis + ' ini?')) {
            document.getElementById('delete-' + jenis + '-' + id).submit();
        }
    }

    // JAVASCRIPT ENGINE AUTOMATIC SLIDESHOW (5 DETIK)
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
        const containers = document.querySelectorAll('.slideshow-container');

        containers.forEach(container => {
            const images = container.querySelectorAll('img');
            if (images.length > 1) {
                let currentIndex = 0;

                setInterval(() => {
                    images[currentIndex].classList.remove('active');
                    currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % images.length;
                    images[currentIndex].classList.add('active');
                }, 5000);
            }
        });
    });
</script>

                        <label for="foto" class="btn-pilih-file">Pilih File</label>
                        <span class="file-name-display" id="fileNameDisplay">Masukkan foto pelatih...</span>
                        <input type="file" id="foto" name="foto[]" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/jpg" style="display:none" onchange="updateFileNameAndPreview(this)" multiple>
                    </div>
                    <div class="form-helper">Format: JPG, PNG. Maks 2MB per file.</div>
                    <div id="imagePreviewContainer" style="display: flex; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 12px;"></div>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="nama">Nama Lengkap</label>
                    <input type="text" id="nama" name="nama" value="{{ old('nama') }}" placeholder="Masukkan nama lengkap pelatih..." required>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="jabatan">Jabatan</label>
                    <select id="jabatan" name="jabatan" class="select-status" required>
                        <option value="" disabled selected hidden>Pilih jabatan pelatih...</option>
                        <option value="Pelatih Tari" {{ old('jabatan') == 'Pelatih Tari' ? 'selected' : '' }}>Pelatih Tari</option>
                        <option value="Pelatih Musik/Gamelan" {{ old('jabatan') == 'Pelatih Musik/Gamelan' ? 'selected' : '' }}>Pelatih Musik/Gamelan</option>
                        <option value="Pelatih Koreografi" {{ old('jabatan') == 'Pelatih Koreografi' ? 'selected' : '' }}>Pelatih Koreografi</option>
                        <option value="Asisten Pelatih" {{ old('jabatan') == 'Asisten Pelatih' ? 'selected' : '' }}>Asisten Pelatih</option>

    <script>
        let kumpulanFilePelatih = [];

        function updateFileNameAndPreview(input) {
            const display = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
            const container = document.getElementById('imagePreviewContainer');
            
            if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
                for (let i = 0; i < input.files.length; i++) {
                    kumpulanFilePelatih.push(input.files[i]);
                }

                container.innerHTML = '';
                const dataTransferObat = new DataTransfer();
                
                kumpulanFilePelatih.forEach((file) => {
                    dataTransferObat.items.add(file);

                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = function(e) {
                        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                        wrapper.style.position = 'relative';
                        wrapper.style.display = 'inline-block';

                        const img = document.createElement('img');
                        img.src = e.target.result;
                        img.style.width = '100px';
                        img.style.height = '100px';
                        img.style.objectFit = 'cover';
                        img.style.borderRadius = '6px';
                        img.style.border = '1px dashed #A87C7C';

                        wrapper.appendChild(img);
                        container.appendChild(wrapper);
                    };
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });

                input.files = dataTransferObat.files;
                display.textContent = kumpulanFilePelatih.length + " file dipilih (terakumulasi)";
                display.style.color = "#333";
            }
        }
    </script>

    <script>
        function openLogoutModal() {
            document.getElementById('modalLogout').style.display = 'flex';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        }

        function closeLogoutModal() {
            document.getElementById('modalLogout').style.display = 'none';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
        }

        function executeLogout() {
            document.getElementById('logout-form').submit();
        }

        window.onclick = function(event) {
            let modal = document.getElementById('modalLogout');
            if (event.target == modal) {
                closeLogoutModal();
            }
        }
    </script>

            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <form id="logout-form" action="{{ route('logout') }}" method="POST" style="display: none;">
        @csrf
    </form>

    <script>
        function openLogoutModal() {
            document.getElementById('modalLogout').style.display = 'flex';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        }
        function closeLogoutModal() {
            document.getElementById('modalLogout').style.display = 'none';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
        }
        function executeLogout() {
            document.getElementById('logout-form').submit();
        }
        window.onclick = function(event) {
            let modal = document.getElementById('modalLogout');
            if (event.target == modal) closeLogoutModal();
        }
        // Toggle sidebar untuk mobile
        document.getElementById('menuToggle')?.addEventListener('click', function() {
            document.querySelector('.sidebar').classList.toggle('open');
        });
    </script>
    @stack('scripts')

        @forelse($beritas as $item)
            @php
                // Tentukan gambar default sebagai cadangan jika tidak ada foto
                $fotoBeritaPath = 'img/images-landing-page/banner_berita.jpeg'; 
                
                if ($item->galeri) {
                    if ($item->galeri instanceof \Illuminate\Support\Collection) {
                        $fotoBeritaPath = $item->galeri->first()->path ?? $fotoBeritaPath;
                    } else {
                        $fotoBeritaPath = $item->galeri->path ?? $fotoBeritaPath;

                        <img src="{{ asset('storage/' . $fotoBeritaPath) }}" alt="{{ $item->judul }}" style="width:100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover;" onerror="this.onerror=null; this.src='{{ asset('img/images-landing-page/banner_berita.jpeg') }}';">
                        <span style="position:absolute; top:15px; left:15px; background:#C0392B; color:#fff; font-size:11px; font-weight:600; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:4px; text-transform:uppercase;">

    <div class="news-pagination">
        @if($beritas->total() > 0)
            <p>Menampilkan {{ $beritas->firstItem() }} - {{ $beritas->lastItem() }} dari {{ $beritas->total() }} berita</p>
        @else

        @endif

            @if($beritas->hasPages())
                {{-- Tombol Sebelumnya --}}

                    <span class="disabled"><i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i></span>
                @else
                    <a href="{{ $beritas->previousPageUrl() }}"><i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i></a>
                @endif

                {{-- Iterasi Angka Halaman --}}
                @foreach ($beritas->getUrlRange(1, $beritas->lastPage()) as $page => $url)
                    @if ($page == $beritas->currentPage())
                        <span class="active">{{ $page }}</span>
                    @else
                        <a href="{{ $url }}">{{ $page }}</a>
                    @endif
                @endforeach

                {{-- Tombol Selanjutnya --}}
                @if ($beritas->hasMorePages())
                    <a href="{{ $beritas->nextPageUrl() }}"><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></a>
                @else
                    <span class="disabled"><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></span>
                @endif
            @endif
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
    function openFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }

    function closeFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'none';
    }

    window.onclick = function(event) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('filterModal');
        if (event.target == modal) {
            closeFilterModal();
        }
    }

    function syncCardVisual(checkboxElement) {
        let parentCard = checkboxElement.closest('.filter-checkbox-card');
        if (checkboxElement.checked) {
            parentCard.classList.add('selected-box');
        } else {
            parentCard.classList.remove('selected-box');
        }
    }

    function submitPublicForm() {
        document.getElementById('formPublicNews').submit();
    }

    document.getElementById('searchPublicNews').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            submitPublicForm();
        }
    });
</script>

                <div class="custom-modal-content">

                                </label>

                                </label>

            </div>

                        </div>
                    </div>

            @if($events->total() > 0)
                <p>Menampilkan {{ $events->firstItem() }} - {{ $events->lastItem() }} dari {{ $events->total() }} event</p>
            @else
                <p>Menampilkan 0 event</p>
            @endif
            
            <div class="news-page-controls">
                @if($events->hasPages())
                    {{-- Sebelumnya --}}
                    @if ($events->onFirstPage())
                        <span class="disabled"><i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i></span>
                    @else
                        <a href="{{ $events->previousPageUrl() }}"><i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i></a>
                    @endif

                    {{-- Loop range nomor halaman --}}
                    @foreach ($events->getUrlRange(1, $events->lastPage()) as $page => $url)
                        @if ($page == $events->currentPage())
                            <span class="active">{{ $page }}</span>
                        @else
                            <a href="{{ $url }}">{{ $page }}</a>
                        @endif
                    @endforeach

                    {{-- Selanjutnya --}}
                    @if ($events->hasMorePages())
                        <a href="{{ $events->nextPageUrl() }}"><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></a>
                    @else
                        <span class="disabled"><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></span>
                    @endif
                @endif
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

</div>

<script>
    function openFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }

    function closeFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'none';
    }

    window.onclick = function(event) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('filterModal');
        if (event.target == modal) {
            closeFilterModal();
        }
    }

    function syncCardVisual(checkboxElement) {
        let parentCard = checkboxElement.closest('.filter-checkbox-card');
        if (checkboxElement.checked) {
            parentCard.classList.add('selected-box');
        } else {
            parentCard.classList.remove('selected-box');
        }
    }

    function submitPublicForm() {
        document.getElementById('formPublicEvent').submit();
    }

    document.getElementById('searchPublicEvent').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            submitPublicForm();
        }
    });
</script>

                                </label>

    <div class="galeri-grid-container">

            {{-- HOTFIX: Otomatis sembunyikan data personel/pembina jika tidak sengaja masuk ke tabel galeri --}}
            @if(
                \Illuminate\Support\Str::contains($item->path, ['personel', 'pembina']) || 
                \Illuminate\Support\Str::contains($item->nama_file, ['pengurus', 'pelatih', 'pembina', 'dummy'])

                {{-- Perbaikan Render Path: Diarahkan ke storage path agar gambar muncul dinamis --}}
                <img src="{{ asset('storage/' . $item->path) }}" alt="{{ $item->nama_file }}" onerror="this.onerror=null; this.src='{{ asset('img/images-landing-page/banner_berita.jpeg') }}';">

                        <a href="{{ $galeriData->previousPageUrl() }}"><i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i></a>
                    @endif

                    {{-- Loop range nomor halaman --}}

                        @if ($page == $galeriData->currentPage())
                            <span class="active">{{ $page }}</span>
                        @else
                            <a href="{{ $url }}">{{ $page }}</a>
                        @endif
                    @endforeach

                    @else
                        <span class="disabled"><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></span>
                    @endif
                @endif
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

</div>

<script>
    function openFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }

    function closeFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'none';
    }

    window.onclick = function(event) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('filterModal');
        if (event.target == modal) {
            closeFilterModal();
        }
    }

    function syncCardVisual(checkboxElement) {
        let parentCard = checkboxElement.closest('.filter-checkbox-card');
        if (checkboxElement.checked) {
            parentCard.classList.add('selected-box');
        } else {
            parentCard.classList.remove('selected-box');
        }
    }

    function submitPublicForm() {
        document.getElementById('formPublicGaleri').submit();
    }

    document.getElementById('searchPublicGaleri').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            submitPublicForm();
        }
    });
</script>

            <p>Menampilkan 0 item kostum</p>
        @endif
        
        <div class="news-page-controls">
            @if($katalogs->hasPages())
                {{-- Sebelumnya --}}
                @if ($katalogs->onFirstPage())
                    <span class="disabled"><i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i></span>
                @else
                    <a href="{{ $katalogs->previousPageUrl() }}"><i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i></a>
                @endif

                {{-- Render Angka Halaman --}}
                @foreach ($katalogs->getUrlRange(1, $katalogs->lastPage()) as $page => $url)
                    @if ($page == $katalogs->currentPage())
                        <span class="active">{{ $page }}</span>
                    @else
                        <a href="{{ $url }}">{{ $page }}</a>
                    @endif
                @endforeach

                {{-- Selanjutnya --}}
                @if ($katalogs->hasMorePages())
                    <a href="{{ $katalogs->nextPageUrl() }}"><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></a>
                @else
                    <span class="disabled"><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></span>
                @endif
            @endif
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
    function openFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'flex';
    }

    // Penjagaan Modal Keluar
    function closeFilterModal() {
        document.getElementById('filterModal').style.display = 'none';
    }

    window.onclick = function(event) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('filterModal');
        if (event.target == modal) {
            closeFilterModal();
        }
    }

    function syncCardVisual(checkboxElement) {
        let parentCard = checkboxElement.closest('.filter-checkbox-card');
        if (checkboxElement.checked) {
            parentCard.classList.add('selected-box');
        } else {
            parentCard.classList.remove('selected-box');
        }
    }

    function submitPublicForm() {
        document.getElementById('formPublicKatalog').submit();
    }

    document.getElementById('searchPublicKatalog').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            submitPublicForm();
        }
    });
</script>

                <p><strong>Sanggar Tari Jawi Etnik Blambangan (JEB)</strong> didirikan sebagai wadah pelestarian seni dan budaya tradisional asli Banyuwangi. Kami berfokus pada pengembangan bakat generasi muda melalui pelatihan tari, musik tradisional, serta pertunjukan seni.</p>
                <p>Dengan pendekatan yang menggabungkan nilai-nilai luhur tradisi dan kreativitas kontemporer, Sanggar JEB terus berkomitmen untuk mengharumkan nama Banyuwangi di berbagai festival.</p>

                <div class="vm-card">

                </div>
                <div class="vm-card">
                    <h3>Misi</h3>

<section class="sejarah-section">

            <div class="sejarah-logo">
                <img src="{{ asset('img/images-landing-page/galeri/logo-jeb.jpg') }}" alt="Logo JEB">
            </div>
            <div class="sejarah-text">
                <h2>Sejarah Sanggar</h2>
                <p>Jiwa Etnik Blambangan (JEB) didirikan pada akhir tahun 2017 oleh Adlin Mustika Alam sebagai wadah pelestarian seni dan budaya etnik, khususnya di wilayah Banyuwangi. JEB hadir dengan konsep pembelajaran yang terstruktur melalui kurikulum berbasis usia dan kemampuan, mulai dari tingkat TK hingga SMA. Pada tahun 2021, JEB resmi terdaftar di Kementerian Hukum dan HAM sebagai lembaga yang legal dan profesional. Saat ini, JEB dipimpin oleh Cinta dan terus berkembang dengan berbagai kegiatan seperti latihan rutin, ujian, pementasan, serta event budaya.</p>
                <p>JEB bertujuan menjadi wadah kreatif bagi generasi muda sekaligus memperkenalkan budaya etnik agar lebih dikenal luas oleh masyarakat.</p>
            </div>

Mari bergabung, berkarya, dan menjaga warisan budaya melalui JEB. Terima kasih atas dukungan Anda dalam menjadikan JEB sebagai wadah kreativitas dan pelestarian budaya."</p>
